result93. Command output
    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]
    max_possible_cover = params["max_possible_cover"]

    model = Model("climate_refuge_reserve_selection")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

    z = {}
    for fid in features:
        for scenario in scenarios:
            sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
            ub = 1.0 if max_possible_cover[(fid, sid)] + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)] else 0.0
            z[(fid, sid)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", ub=ub, name=f"z[{fid},{sid}]")

    floor_ub = sum(fdata["conservation_value"] for fdata in features.values())
    worst_floor = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=floor_ub, vtype="C", name="worst_floor")

    add_common_constraints(model, x, instance, params)

    score_expr = {}
    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        for fid in features:
            coverage_terms = [
                base_link * quality[(pid, action_name, sid)] * x[(pid, action_name)]
                for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, [])
                for action_name in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][pid]
            ]
            lhs = quicksum(coverage_terms) if coverage_terms else 0.0
            model.addCons(
                lhs >= target[(fid, sid)] * z[(fid, sid)],
                name=f"coverage[{fid},{sid}]",
            )

        score_expr[sid] = quicksum(
            features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in features
        )
        model.addCons(worst_floor <= score_expr[sid], name=f"floor[{sid}]")

        priority_rhs = sum(
            features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
            for fid in priority_features
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in priority_features)
            >= priority_rhs,
            name=f"priority_value[{sid}]",
        )

    expected_score_expr = quicksum(
        scenario["probability"] * score_expr[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    cost_expr = quicksum(
        (capital_cost[key] + maintenance_cost[key]) * x[key] for key in eligible
    )
    objective = config["benefit_scale"] * (
        expected_score_expr + config["robust_floor_weight"] * worst_floor
    ) - config["cost_scale"] * cost_expr
    model.setObjective(objective, sense="maximize")

    return model, x, z, score_expr


def build_phase1_model(instance: Dict, params: Dict, time_limit: float) -> Tuple[Model, Dict]:
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]
    max_possible_cover = params["max_possible_cover"]

    model = Model("climate_refuge_phase1")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

    add_common_constraints(model, x, instance, params)

    z = {}
    slack = {}
    theta = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name="theta")
    priority_rhs = sum(
        features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        for fid in priority_features
    )

    priority_score_expr = {}
    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        for fid in priority_features:
            ub = 1.0 if max_possible_cover[(fid, sid)] + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)] else 0.0
            z[(fid, sid)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", ub=ub, name=f"zp[{fid},{sid}]")
            coverage_terms = [
                base_link * quality[(pid, action_name, sid)] * x[(pid, action_name)]
                for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, [])
                for action_name in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][pid]
            ]
            lhs = quicksum(coverage_terms) if coverage_terms else 0.0
            model.addCons(
                lhs >= target[(fid, sid)] * z[(fid, sid)],
                name=f"priority_coverage[{fid},{sid}]",
            )

        priority_score_expr[sid] = quicksum(
            features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in priority_features
        )
        slack[sid] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name=f"slack[{sid}]")
        model.addCons(priority_score_expr[sid] + slack[sid] >= priority_rhs, name=f"priority_slack[{sid}]")
        model.addCons(theta >= slack[sid], name=f"theta_lb[{sid}]")

    expected_priority = quicksum(
        scenario["probability"] * priority_score_expr[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    model.setObjective(
        1_000_000.0 * theta
        + 1_000.0 * quicksum(slack[s["scenario_id"]] for s in scenarios)
        - expected_priority,
        sense="minimize",
    )
    return model, x


def extract_solution(model: Model, xvars: Dict) -> Dict[str, str]:
    if model.getNSols() <= 0 or model.getBestSol() is None:
        raise RuntimeError("SCIP finished without an incumbent solution.")
    selected = {}
    for (pid, action_name), var in xvars.items():
        if model.getVal(var) > 0.5:
            selected[pid] = action_name
    return dict(sorted(selected.items()))


def write_solution(solution: Dict[str, str]) -> None:
    with open(SOLUTION_PATH, "w", newline="") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow(["parcel_id", "action"])
        for pid, action in sorted(solution.items()):
            writer.writerow([pid, action])


def read_solution(path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
    with open(path, "r", newline="") as fh:
        reader = csv.DictReader(fh)
        if reader.fieldnames != ["parcel_id", "action"]:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected columns in solution file: {reader.fieldnames}")
        solution = {}
        for row in reader:
            pid = row["parcel_id"]
            action = row["action"]
            if pid in solution:
                raise ValueError(f"Duplicate parcel row in solution: {pid}")
            solution[pid] = action
    return solution


def audit_solution(instance: Dict, params: Dict, solution: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict:
    config = instance["config"]
    parcels = instance["parcels"]
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    regions = instance["regions"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    conflicts = instance["conflicts"]
    eligible_by_parcel = instance["eligible_by_parcel"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]

    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    staff_cost = params["staff_cost"]
    nursery_cost = params["nursery_cost"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]

    selected_pairs = []
    for pid, action in solution.items():
        if pid not in parcels:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown parcel in solution: {pid}")
        if action not in eligible_by_parcel.get(pid, []):
            raise ValueError(f"Action {action} is not allowed for parcel {pid}")
        selected_pairs.append((pid, action))

    residuals = {}
    violated_groups = set()

    total_selected = len(selected_pairs)
    residuals["min_selected"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        total_selected, float(config["minimum_selected_parcels"])
    )
    residuals["max_selected"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        total_selected, float(config["maximum_selected_parcels"])
    )
    if residuals["min_selected"] > FEAS_TOL or residuals["max_selected"] > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("selected_count")

    capital = sum(capital_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    maintenance = sum(maintenance_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    staff = sum(staff_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    nursery = sum(nursery_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    corridor = sum(parcels[pid]["migration_corridor"] for pid, _ in selected_pairs)

    residuals["capital_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(capital, float(config["capital_budget"]))
    residuals["maintenance_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        maintenance, float(config["annual_maintenance_budget"])
    )
    residuals["total_staff_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        staff, float(config["staff_day_budget"])
    )
    residuals["nursery_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        nursery, float(config["nursery_unit_budget"])
    )
    residuals["corridor_min"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        corridor, float(config["minimum_migration_corridors"])
    )

    for key in [
        "capital_budget",
        "maintenance_budget",
        "total_staff_budget",
        "nursery_budget",
        "corridor_min",
    ]:
        if residuals[key] > FEAS_TOL:
            violated_groups.add(key)

    action_counts = defaultdict(int)
    for _, action in selected_pairs:
        action_counts[action] += 1
    residuals["min_protect_actions"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        action_counts["protect"], float(config["minimum_protect_actions"])
    )
    residuals["min_easement_actions"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        action_counts["migration_easement"], float(config["minimum_migration_easement_actions"])
    )
    residuals["max_restore_actions"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        action_counts["restore"], float(config["maximum_restore_actions"])
    )
    for key in ["min_protect_actions", "min_easement_actions", "max_restore_actions"]:
        if residuals[key] > FEAS_TOL:
            violated_groups.add(key)

    selected_parcels = set(solution)

    max_conflict_resid = 0.0
    for p1, p2, _ in conflicts:
        lhs = float(p1 in selected_parcels) + float(p2 in selected_parcels)
        max_conflict_resid = max(max_conflict_resid, _norm_residual_lte(lhs, 1.0))

    if max_conflict_resid > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("conflicts")

    region_public = defaultdict(int)
    region_staff = defaultdict(float)
    for pid, action in selected_pairs:
        region = parcels[pid]["region"]
        region_counts[region] += 1
        region_public[region] += parcels[pid]["public_access"]
        region_staff[region] += staff_cost[(pid, action)]

    region_residuals = {}
    for region, rdata in regions.items():
        region_residuals[f"{region}_min_selected"] = _norm_residual_gte(
            region_counts[region], float(rdata["min_selected"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_max_selected"] = _norm_residual_lte(
            region_counts[region], float(rdata["max_selected"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_min_public"] = _norm_residual_gte(
            region_public[region], float(rdata["min_public_access"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_staff_cap"] = _norm_residual_lte(
            region_staff[region], float(rdata["staff_day_cap"])
        )
    residuals.update(region_residuals)
    if any(value > FEAS_TOL for value in region_residuals.values()):
        violated_groups.add("region_requirements")

    coverage = {}
    covered = {}
    scenario_scores = {}
    priority_requirement = sum(
        features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        for fid in priority_features
    )
    priority_residual = 0.0

    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        score = 0.0
        priority_score = 0.0
        for fid in features:
            total = 0.0
            for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, []):
                action = solution.get(pid)
                if action is None:
                    continue
                total += base_link * quality[(pid, action, sid)]
            coverage[(fid, sid)] = total
            is_covered = total + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)]
            covered[(fid, sid)] = int(is_covered)
            if is_covered:
                score += features[fid]["conservation_value"]
                if fid in priority_features:
                    priority_score += features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        scenario_scores[sid] = score
        priority_residual = max(priority_residual, _norm_residual_gte(priority_score, priority_requirement))

    residuals["priority_requirement"] = priority_residual
    if priority_residual > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("priority_requirement")

    expected_score = sum(
        scenario["probability"] * scenario_scores[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    worst_floor = min(scenario_scores.values()) if scenario_scores else 0.0
    objective = config["benefit_scale"] * (
        expected_score + config["robust_floor_weight"] * worst_floor
    ) - config["cost_scale"] * (capital + maintenance)

    max_normalized_residual = max(residuals.values()) if residuals else 0.0
    integrality_residual = 0.0
    decision_universe_coverage = len(solution) / max(1, len(instance["parcels"]))

    return {
        "selected_count": total_selected,
        "capital": capital,
        "maintenance": maintenance,
        "staff": staff,
        "nursery": nursery,
        "corridor_count": corridor,
        "action_counts": dict(action_counts),
        "scenario_scores": scenario_scores,
        "expected_score": expected_score,
        "worst_floor": worst_floor,
        "objective": objective,
        "priority_requirement_rhs": priority_requirement,
        "max_normalized_residual": max_normalized_residual,
        "violated_constraint_groups": sorted(violated_groups),
        "violated_group_count": len(violated_groups),
        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
        "decision_universe_coverage": decision_universe_coverage,
        "residuals": residuals,
        "coverage": coverage,
        "covered": covered,
    }


def write_log(
    command: str,
    time_limit: float,
    runtime: float,
    status: str,
    nsols: int,
    solver_obj: float,
    dual_bound: float,
    gap: float,
    audit: Dict,
) -> None:
    lines = [
        "# Solve Log",
        "",
        "## Commands",
        "",
        f"- `PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python -c \"import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)\"`",
        f"- `{command}`",
        "",
        "## Solver",
        "",
        f"- Status: `{status}`",
        f"- Time limit seconds: `{_float_fmt(time_limit)}`",
        f"- Runtime seconds: `{_float_fmt(runtime)}`",
        f"- Incumbent count: `{nsols}`",
        f"- Solver objective: `{_float_fmt(solver_obj)}`",
        f"- Dual bound: `{_float_fmt(dual_bound)}`",
        f"- Relative gap: `{_float_fmt(gap)}`",
        "",
        "## Validation",
        "",
        f"- Selected parcels: `{audit['selected_count']}`",
        f"- Capital used: `{_float_fmt(audit['capital'])}`",
        f"- Maintenance used: `{_float_fmt(audit['maintenance'])}`",
        f"- Staff used: `{_float_fmt(audit['staff'])}`",
        f"- Nursery units used: `{_float_fmt(audit['nursery'])}`",
        f"- Corridor parcels selected: `{audit['corridor_count']}`",
        f"- Expected covered value: `{_float_fmt(audit['expected_score'])}`",
        f"- Worst-scenario covered value: `{_float_fmt(audit['worst_floor'])}`",
        f"- Recomputed objective: `{_float_fmt(audit['objective'])}`",
        f"- Max normalized hard-constraint residual: `{_float_fmt(audit['max_normalized_residual'])}`",
        f"- Violated constraint groups: `{audit['violated_group_count']}`",
        f"- Integrality residual: `{_float_fmt(audit['integrality_residual'])}`",
        f"- Decision universe coverage: `{_float_fmt(audit['decision_universe_coverage'])}`",
        "",
        "## Action Counts",
        "",
    ]

    for action_name in ["protect", "restore", "migration_easement"]:
        lines.append(f"- `{action_name}`: `{audit['action_counts'].get(action_name, 0)}`")

    lines.extend(["", "## Violations", ""])
    if audit["violated_constraint_groups"]:
        for group in audit["violated_constraint_groups"]:
            lines.append(f"- `{group}`")
    else:
        lines.append("- None")

    lines.extend(["", "## Scenario Scores", ""])
    for sid in sorted(audit["scenario_scores"]):
        lines.append(f"- `{sid}`: `{_float_fmt(audit['scenario_scores'][sid])}`")

    with open(LOG_PATH, "w", newline="") as fh:
        fh.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

    start = time.time()
    command = "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python /app/submissions/solve.py"

        raise RuntimeError("SCIP did not produce a feasible incumbent within the time limit.")

    gap = model.getGap()

        runtime=runtime,
        status=status,
        nsols=nsols,
        solver_obj=solver_obj,
        dual_bound=dual_bound,
        gap=gap,
        audit=audit,
    )

        raise RuntimeError(

---
name: optimization-modeling-core
description: Use for natural-language-to-optimization tasks that require translating a business problem into a complete mathematical model before writing solver code.
metadata:
  short-description: Turn business text into a full optimization model
---

# Optimization Modeling Core

## Use when

- The user gives a word problem, business process, paper scenario, or benchmark prompt and expects a formal optimization model.
- Solver code exists but the issue appears to be a missing or incorrect formulation.
- The task asks for a complete model, not only a heuristic, simulation, or data summary.

## Do not use when

- The task is purely about software plumbing, visualization, data cleaning, or report writing.
- The problem is already fully formulated and the user only asks for code translation into a specific solver; use the solver-specific skill as the primary guide.
- The request is exploratory and the user explicitly wants brainstorming without implementation.

## Model-first workflow

1. Restate the business decision in plain language.
2. Identify sets, indices, units, and time granularity.
3. Extract parameters from data files and confirm units.
4. Define decision variables with domains and business meaning.
5. Write the objective with every cost, reward, penalty, and service term.
6. Write constraints grouped by operational rule.
7. Check edge cases: missing eligibility, impossible demand, fixed commitments, and terminal-state requirements.
8. Only then implement code.

## Ambiguity handling

- If the brief is ambiguous but data and examples imply a convention, state the assumption and continue.
- If ambiguity changes feasibility, objective direction, or deliverable schema, ask before coding or implement the least risky interpretation with a clear note.
- If units conflict across files, normalize units explicitly before modeling.
- If a hard requirement conflicts with data, diagnose the conflict before adding slack variables.

## Formulation checklist

- Every row in an input table should either become a parameter, an eligibility rule, or be explicitly irrelevant.
- Every decision variable should appear in at least one binding constraint and usually in the objective.
- Demand balance should use the correct equality or inequality direction; do not weaken equality to `>=` unless surplus is genuinely allowed.
- Capacity constraints should include activation/open/setup binaries when the business says inactive resources cannot produce, ship, or serve.
- Shortage, backlog, overtime, outsourcing, and postponement variables need explicit cost and bounds.
- Multi-period models need state transition equations and initial/terminal states.
- If the user asks for explanation, describe the modeling logic in business terms after solving.

## Constraint boundary rules

- Hard constraints represent physical impossibility, policy mandates, legal requirements, or explicit benchmark feasibility rules.
- Soft constraints need penalty variables or objective terms; do not silently turn hard constraints into penalties.
- Service levels should specify numerator, denominator, eligible population, and time window.
- Fairness constraints should define the comparison group and whether unavailable resources count.
- Logical implications need both directions only when the business rule says equivalence, not merely implication.

## Objective boundary rules

- Confirm whether the problem minimizes cost, maximizes profit, maximizes service, or uses lexicographic priorities.
- Do not mix penalties with primary economics unless their scale is intentional.
- Include fixed costs only once per activated entity.
- Include revenue only for fulfilled demand or accepted jobs, not for unmet or canceled work.
- Keep reporting metrics separate from optimized objective terms unless the prompt explicitly optimizes them.

## Implementation expectations

- Use structured parsing for CSV/JSON instead of manual string manipulation.
- Keep solver code, solution export, and validation code clearly separated.
- For benchmark or task environments, read the provided brief before coding and follow the expected output schema exactly.
- When comparing two answers, diagnose concrete formulation differences and objective consequences, not only feasibility.
- Write exported solutions at the precision expected by the checker; avoid unnecessary rounding.
- Preserve the input schema and output schema unless the user asks to redesign the benchmark.

## Verification boundary

- Verify feasibility independently from solver status when an evaluator or checker exists.
- Recompute objective from exported artifacts, not only from in-memory solver expressions.
- For benchmark tasks, create at least one negative check for each newly added rule when practical.
- For paper or real-world tasks, separate what is directly supported by the source from synthetic or assumed data.

## Red flags

- Objective terms that are counted twice or charged at the wrong marginal rate.
- Binary variables not linked tightly enough to continuous activity.
- Time periods shifted by one index.
- Average or fairness constraints that ignore the denominator or eligibility set.
- Public prompt files that reveal solver-ready variables or formulas when the benchmark is meant to test modeling translation.
- A feasible solution that exploits missing eligibility, free disposal, negative cycles, or unbounded production.
- A model that reports optimality after a time limit, infeasible status, or missing incumbent.

---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

        capital_cost[key] / max(1.0, float(config["capital_budget"])) * x[key] for key in eligible
    )
    norm_cost += quicksum(
        maintenance_cost[key] / max(1.0, float(config["annual_maintenance_budget"])) * x[key]
        for key in eligible
    )
    norm_cost += quicksum(

    )
    norm_cost += quicksum(
        nursery_cost[key] / max(1.0, float(config["nursery_unit_budget"])) * x[key] for key in eligible
    )
    norm_cost += 0.001 * quicksum(x[key] for key in eligible)
    model.setObjective(norm_cost, sense="minimize")
    return model, x


def build_priority_repair_model(
    instance: Dict, params: Dict, candidate: Dict[str, str], time_limit: float
) -> Tuple[Model, Dict, Dict, Dict, object, float]:
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]
    max_possible_cover = params["max_possible_cover"]

    model = Model("priority_repair")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

    add_common_constraints(model, x, instance, params)

    z = {}
    slack = {}
    theta = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name="theta")
    priority_rhs = sum(
        features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        for fid in priority_features
    )
    priority_rhs = max(priority_rhs, FEAS_TOL)

    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        for fid in priority_features:
            ub = 1.0 if max_possible_cover[(fid, sid)] + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)] else 0.0
            z[(fid, sid)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", ub=ub, name=f"zp[{fid},{sid}]")
            coverage_terms = [
                base_link * quality[(pid, action_name, sid)] * x[(pid, action_name)]
                for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, [])
                for action_name in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][pid]
            ]
            lhs = quicksum(coverage_terms) if coverage_terms else 0.0
            model.addCons(
                lhs >= target[(fid, sid)] * z[(fid, sid)],
                name=f"priority_coverage[{fid},{sid}]",
            )

        slack[sid] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name=f"slack[{sid}]")
        priority_score = quicksum(
            features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in priority_features
        )
        model.addCons(priority_score + slack[sid] >= priority_rhs, name=f"priority_slack[{sid}]")
        model.addCons(theta >= slack[sid] / priority_rhs, name=f"theta_lb[{sid}]")

    distance_expr = quicksum(
        (1.0 - x[(pid, action_name)]) if candidate.get(pid) == action_name else x[(pid, action_name)]
        for pid, action_name in eligible
    )
    total_norm_slack = quicksum(slack[s["scenario_id"]] / priority_rhs for s in scenarios)
    model.setObjective(1_000_000.0 * theta + 10_000.0 * total_norm_slack + distance_expr, sense="minimize")
    return model, x, z, slack, theta, priority_rhs


def extract_solution(model: Model, xvars: Dict) -> Dict[str, str]:
    if model.getNSols() <= 0 or model.getBestSol() is None:
        raise RuntimeError("SCIP finished without an incumbent solution.")
    selected = {}
    for (pid, action_name), var in xvars.items():
        if model.getVal(var) > 0.5:
            selected[pid] = action_name
    return dict(sorted(selected.items()))


def add_warm_start(
    model: Model, xvars: Dict, zvars: Dict, worst_floor_var, audit: Dict, solution: Dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    sol = model.createSol()
    for (pid, action_name), var in xvars.items():
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, 1.0 if solution.get(pid) == action_name else 0.0)
    for key, var in zvars.items():
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, float(audit["covered"][key]))
    model.setSolVal(sol, worst_floor_var, audit["worst_floor"])
    model.addSol(sol, free=True)


def add_priority_repair_warm_start(
    model: Model,
    xvars: Dict,
    zvars: Dict,
    slackvars: Dict,
    theta_var,
    candidate_audit: Dict,
    solution: Dict[str, str],
    features: Dict,
    priority_features: List[str],
    priority_rhs: float,
) -> None:
    sol = model.createSol()
    for (pid, action_name), var in xvars.items():
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, 1.0 if solution.get(pid) == action_name else 0.0)
    for (fid, sid), var in zvars.items():
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, float(candidate_audit["covered"][(fid, sid)]))
    theta_val = 0.0
    for sid, var in slackvars.items():
        priority_value = 0.0
        for fid in priority_features:
            if candidate_audit["covered"][(fid, sid)]:
                priority_value += features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        slack = max(0.0, priority_rhs - priority_value)
        theta_val = max(theta_val, slack / max(priority_rhs, FEAS_TOL))
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, slack)
    model.setSolVal(sol, theta_var, theta_val)
    model.addSol(sol, free=True)


def write_solution(solution: Dict[str, str]) -> None:
    with open(SOLUTION_PATH, "w", newline="") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow(["parcel_id", "action"])
        for pid, action in sorted(solution.items()):
            writer.writerow([pid, action])


def read_solution(path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
    with open(path, "r", newline="") as fh:
        reader = csv.DictReader(fh)
        if reader.fieldnames != ["parcel_id", "action"]:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected columns in solution file: {reader.fieldnames}")
        solution = {}
        for row in reader:
            pid = row["parcel_id"]
            action = row["action"]
            if pid in solution:
                raise ValueError(f"Duplicate parcel row in solution: {pid}")
            solution[pid] = action
    return solution


def audit_solution(instance: Dict, params: Dict, solution: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict:
    config = instance["config"]
    parcels = instance["parcels"]
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    regions = instance["regions"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    conflicts = instance["conflicts"]
    eligible_by_parcel = instance["eligible_by_parcel"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]

    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    staff_cost = params["staff_cost"]
    nursery_cost = params["nursery_cost"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]

    selected_pairs = []
    for pid, action in solution.items():
        if pid not in parcels:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown parcel in solution: {pid}")
        if action not in eligible_by_parcel.get(pid, []):
            raise ValueError(f"Action {action} is not allowed for parcel {pid}")
        selected_pairs.append((pid, action))

    residuals = {}
    violated_groups = set()

    total_selected = len(selected_pairs)
    residuals["min_selected"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        total_selected, float(config["minimum_selected_parcels"])
    )
    residuals["max_selected"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        total_selected, float(config["maximum_selected_parcels"])
    )
    if residuals["min_selected"] > FEAS_TOL or residuals["max_selected"] > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("selected_count")

    capital = sum(capital_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    maintenance = sum(maintenance_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    staff = sum(staff_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    nursery = sum(nursery_cost[key] for key in selected_pairs)
    corridor = sum(parcels[pid]["migration_corridor"] for pid, _ in selected_pairs)

    residuals["capital_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(capital, float(config["capital_budget"]))
    residuals["maintenance_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        maintenance, float(config["annual_maintenance_budget"])
    )
    residuals["total_staff_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        staff, float(config["staff_day_budget"])
    )
    residuals["nursery_budget"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        nursery, float(config["nursery_unit_budget"])
    )
    residuals["corridor_min"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        corridor, float(config["minimum_migration_corridors"])
    )

    for key in [
        "capital_budget",
        "maintenance_budget",
        "total_staff_budget",
        "nursery_budget",
        "corridor_min",
    ]:
        if residuals[key] > FEAS_TOL:
            violated_groups.add(key)

    action_counts = defaultdict(int)
    for _, action in selected_pairs:
        action_counts[action] += 1
    residuals["min_protect_actions"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        action_counts["protect"], float(config["minimum_protect_actions"])
    )
    residuals["min_easement_actions"] = _norm_residual_gte(
        action_counts["migration_easement"], float(config["minimum_migration_easement_actions"])
    )
    residuals["max_restore_actions"] = _norm_residual_lte(
        action_counts["restore"], float(config["maximum_restore_actions"])
    )
    for key in ["min_protect_actions", "min_easement_actions", "max_restore_actions"]:
        if residuals[key] > FEAS_TOL:
            violated_groups.add(key)

    selected_parcels = set(solution)
    max_conflict_resid = 0.0
    for p1, p2, _ in conflicts:
        lhs = float(p1 in selected_parcels) + float(p2 in selected_parcels)
        max_conflict_resid = max(max_conflict_resid, _norm_residual_lte(lhs, 1.0))
    residuals["conflicts"] = max_conflict_resid
    if max_conflict_resid > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("conflicts")

    region_counts = defaultdict(int)
    region_public = defaultdict(int)
    region_staff = defaultdict(float)
    for pid, action in selected_pairs:
        region = parcels[pid]["region"]
        region_counts[region] += 1
        region_public[region] += parcels[pid]["public_access"]
        region_staff[region] += staff_cost[(pid, action)]

    region_residuals = {}
    for region, rdata in regions.items():
        region_residuals[f"{region}_min_selected"] = _norm_residual_gte(
            region_counts[region], float(rdata["min_selected"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_max_selected"] = _norm_residual_lte(
            region_counts[region], float(rdata["max_selected"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_min_public"] = _norm_residual_gte(
            region_public[region], float(rdata["min_public_access"])
        )
        region_residuals[f"{region}_staff_cap"] = _norm_residual_lte(
            region_staff[region], float(rdata["staff_day_cap"])
        )
    residuals.update(region_residuals)
    if any(value > FEAS_TOL for value in region_residuals.values()):
        violated_groups.add("region_requirements")

    coverage = {}
    covered = {}
    scenario_scores = {}
    priority_requirement = sum(
        features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        for fid in priority_features
    )
    priority_residual = 0.0

    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        score = 0.0
        priority_score = 0.0
        for fid in features:
            total = 0.0
            for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, []):
                action = solution.get(pid)
                if action is None:
                    continue
                total += base_link * quality[(pid, action, sid)]
            coverage[(fid, sid)] = total
            is_covered = total + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)]
            covered[(fid, sid)] = int(is_covered)
            if is_covered:
                score += features[fid]["conservation_value"]
                if fid in priority_features:
                    priority_score += features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        scenario_scores[sid] = score
        priority_residual = max(priority_residual, _norm_residual_gte(priority_score, priority_requirement))

    residuals["priority_requirement"] = priority_residual
    if priority_residual > FEAS_TOL:
        violated_groups.add("priority_requirement")

    expected_score = sum(
        scenario["probability"] * scenario_scores[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    worst_floor = min(scenario_scores.values()) if scenario_scores else 0.0
    objective = config["benefit_scale"] * (
        expected_score + config["robust_floor_weight"] * worst_floor
    ) - config["cost_scale"] * (capital + maintenance)

    max_normalized_residual = max(residuals.values()) if residuals else 0.0
    integrality_residual = 0.0
    decision_universe_coverage = len(solution) / max(1, len(instance["parcels"]))

    return {
        "selected_count": total_selected,
        "capital": capital,
        "maintenance": maintenance,
        "staff": staff,
        "nursery": nursery,
        "corridor_count": corridor,
        "action_counts": dict(action_counts),
        "scenario_scores": scenario_scores,
        "expected_score": expected_score,
        "worst_floor": worst_floor,
        "objective": objective,
        "priority_requirement_rhs": priority_requirement,
        "max_normalized_residual": max_normalized_residual,
        "violated_constraint_groups": sorted(violated_groups),
        "violated_group_count": len(violated_groups),
        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
        "decision_universe_coverage": decision_universe_coverage,
        "residuals": residuals,
        "coverage": coverage,
        "covered": covered,
    }

def add_common_constraints(model: Model, x: Dict, instance: Dict, params: Dict) -> None:
    config = instance["config"]
    parcels = instance["parcels"]
    regions = instance["regions"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    conflicts = instance["conflicts"]
    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    staff_cost = params["staff_cost"]
    nursery_cost = params["nursery_cost"]

    for pid, allowed in instance["eligible_by_parcel"].items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x[(pid, action_name)] for action_name in allowed) <= 1,
            name=f"one_action[{pid}]",
        )

    total_selected = quicksum(x[key] for key in eligible)
    model.addCons(total_selected >= int(config["minimum_selected_parcels"]), name="min_selected")
    model.addCons(total_selected <= int(config["maximum_selected_parcels"]), name="max_selected")

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(capital_cost[key] * x[key] for key in eligible) <= float(config["capital_budget"]),
        name="capital_budget",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(maintenance_cost[key] * x[key] for key in eligible)
        <= float(config["annual_maintenance_budget"]),
        name="maintenance_budget",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(staff_cost[key] * x[key] for key in eligible) <= float(config["staff_day_budget"]),
        name="total_staff_budget",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(nursery_cost[key] * x[key] for key in eligible)
        <= float(config["nursery_unit_budget"]),
        name="nursery_budget",
    )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(x[(pid, "protect")] for pid, action_name in eligible if action_name == "protect")
        >= int(config["minimum_protect_actions"]),
        name="min_protect_actions",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(
            x[(pid, "migration_easement")]
            for pid, action_name in eligible
            if action_name == "migration_easement"
        )
        >= int(config["minimum_migration_easement_actions"]),
        name="min_easement_actions",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(x[(pid, "restore")] for pid, action_name in eligible if action_name == "restore")
        <= int(config["maximum_restore_actions"]),
        name="max_restore_actions",
    )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(
            parcels[pid]["migration_corridor"] * x[(pid, action_name)]
            for pid, action_name in eligible
        )
        >= int(config["minimum_migration_corridors"]),
        name="min_corridors",
    )

    for region, rdata in regions.items():
        keys = [key for key in eligible if parcels[key[0]]["region"] == region]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x[key] for key in keys) >= rdata["min_selected"],
            name=f"region_min_selected[{region}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x[key] for key in keys) <= rdata["max_selected"],
            name=f"region_max_selected[{region}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(parcels[key[0]]["public_access"] * x[key] for key in keys)
            >= rdata["min_public_access"],
            name=f"region_min_public[{region}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(staff_cost[key] * x[key] for key in keys) <= rdata["staff_day_cap"],
            name=f"region_staff_cap[{region}]",
        )

    for p1, p2, reason in conflicts:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x[(p1, a)] for a in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][p1])
            + quicksum(x[(p2, a)] for a in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][p2])
            <= 1,
            name=f"conflict[{p1},{p2},{reason}]",
        )


def build_model(instance: Dict, params: Dict, time_limit: float) -> Tuple[Model, Dict, Dict, Dict]:
    config = instance["config"]
    parcels = instance["parcels"]
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]

    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]
    max_possible_cover = params["max_possible_cover"]

    model = Model("climate_refuge_reserve_selection")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

    z = {}
    for fid in features:
        for scenario in scenarios:
            sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
            ub = 1.0 if max_possible_cover[(fid, sid)] + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)] else 0.0
            z[(fid, sid)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", ub=ub, name=f"z[{fid},{sid}]")

    floor_ub = sum(fdata["conservation_value"] for fdata in features.values())
    worst_floor = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=floor_ub, vtype="C", name="worst_floor")

    add_common_constraints(model, x, instance, params)

    score_expr = {}
    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        for fid in features:
            coverage_terms = [
                base_link * quality[(pid, action_name, sid)] * x[(pid, action_name)]
                for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, [])
                for action_name in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][pid]
            ]
            lhs = quicksum(coverage_terms) if coverage_terms else 0.0
            model.addCons(
                lhs >= target[(fid, sid)] * z[(fid, sid)],
                name=f"coverage[{fid},{sid}]",
            )

        score_expr[sid] = quicksum(
            features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in features
        )
        model.addCons(worst_floor <= score_expr[sid], name=f"floor[{sid}]")

        priority_rhs = sum(
            features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
            for fid in priority_features
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in priority_features)
            >= priority_rhs,
            name=f"priority_value[{sid}]",
        )

    expected_score_expr = quicksum(
        scenario["probability"] * score_expr[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    cost_expr = quicksum(
        (capital_cost[key] + maintenance_cost[key]) * x[key] for key in eligible
    )
    objective = config["benefit_scale"] * (
        expected_score_expr + config["robust_floor_weight"] * worst_floor
    ) - config["cost_scale"] * cost_expr
    model.setObjective(objective, sense="maximize")

    return model, x, z, score_expr


def build_phase1_model(instance: Dict, params: Dict, time_limit: float) -> Tuple[Model, Dict]:
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]
    priority_features = instance["priority_features"]
    quality = params["quality"]
    target = params["target"]
    max_possible_cover = params["max_possible_cover"]

    model = Model("climate_refuge_phase1")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

    add_common_constraints(model, x, instance, params)

    z = {}
    slack = {}
    theta = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name="theta")
    priority_rhs = sum(
        features[fid]["priority_min_rate"] * features[fid]["conservation_value"]
        for fid in priority_features
    )

    priority_score_expr = {}
    for scenario in scenarios:
        sid = scenario["scenario_id"]
        for fid in priority_features:
            ub = 1.0 if max_possible_cover[(fid, sid)] + FEAS_TOL >= target[(fid, sid)] else 0.0
            z[(fid, sid)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", ub=ub, name=f"zp[{fid},{sid}]")
            coverage_terms = [
                base_link * quality[(pid, action_name, sid)] * x[(pid, action_name)]
                for pid, base_link in links_by_feature.get(fid, [])
                for action_name in instance["eligible_by_parcel"][pid]
            ]
            lhs = quicksum(coverage_terms) if coverage_terms else 0.0
            model.addCons(
                lhs >= target[(fid, sid)] * z[(fid, sid)],
                name=f"priority_coverage[{fid},{sid}]",
            )

        priority_score_expr[sid] = quicksum(
            features[fid]["conservation_value"] * z[(fid, sid)] for fid in priority_features
        )
        slack[sid] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, vtype="C", name=f"slack[{sid}]")
        model.addCons(priority_score_expr[sid] + slack[sid] >= priority_rhs, name=f"priority_slack[{sid}]")
        model.addCons(theta >= slack[sid], name=f"theta_lb[{sid}]")

    expected_priority = quicksum(
        scenario["probability"] * priority_score_expr[scenario["scenario_id"]] for scenario in scenarios
    )
    model.setObjective(
        1_000_000.0 * theta
        + 1_000.0 * quicksum(slack[s["scenario_id"]] for s in scenarios)
        - expected_priority,
        sense="minimize",
    )
    return model, x


def build_base_feasibility_model(instance: Dict, params: Dict, time_limit: float) -> Tuple[Model, Dict]:
    config = instance["config"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    capital_cost = params["capital_cost"]
    maintenance_cost = params["maintenance_cost"]
    staff_cost = params["staff_cost"]
    nursery_cost = params["nursery_cost"]

    model = Model("base_feasibility")
    model.hideOutput()
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/randomseedshift", 0)
    model.setParam("randomization/permutationseed", 0)

    x = {}
    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        x[(pid, action_name)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{pid},{action_name}]")

import csv
import json
import math
import os
import sys
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum


BASE_DIR = "/app"
DATA_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "data")
SUBMISSIONS_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "submissions")
SOLUTION_PATH = os.path.join(SUBMISSIONS_DIR, "solution.csv")
LOG_PATH = os.path.join(SUBMISSIONS_DIR, "solve_log.md")

REL_GAP = 0.0005
MAX_SINGLE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS = 300.0
FEAS_TOL = 1e-6


def _read_csv(path: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    with open(path, "r", newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def _float_fmt(value: float) -> str:
    if value is None or (isinstance(value, float) and (math.isinf(value) or math.isnan(value))):
        return "n/a"
    return f"{value:.8f}"


def _norm_residual_lte(lhs: float, rhs: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, lhs - rhs) / max(1.0, abs(rhs))


def _norm_residual_gte(lhs: float, rhs: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, rhs - lhs) / max(1.0, abs(rhs))


def get_time_limit() -> float:
    env_value = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if env_value is None:
        return MAX_SINGLE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS
    try:
        parsed = float(env_value)
    except ValueError:
        return MAX_SINGLE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS
    return max(1.0, min(MAX_SINGLE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS, parsed))


def load_instance() -> Dict:
    with open(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "config.json"), "r") as fh:
        config = json.load(fh)

    action_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "actions.csv"))
    parcel_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "parcels.csv"))
    feature_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "features.csv"))
    scenario_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "scenarios.csv"))
    conflict_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "parcel_conflicts.csv"))
    region_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "region_requirements.csv"))
    link_rows = _read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "parcel_feature_habitat.csv"))

    actions = {}
    for row in action_rows:
        act = row["action"]
        actions[act] = {
            "fixed_cost_multiplier": float(row["fixed_cost_multiplier"]),
            "maintenance_multiplier": float(row["maintenance_multiplier"]),
            "staff_multiplier": float(row["staff_multiplier"]),
            "habitat_multiplier": float(row["habitat_multiplier"]),
            "upland_multiplier": float(row["upland_multiplier"]),
            "nursery_units": float(row["nursery_units"]),
            "public_access_required": int(row["public_access_required"]),
        }

    parcels = {}
    for row in parcel_rows:
        pid = row["parcel_id"]
        parcels[pid] = {
            "region": row["region"],
            "owner_type": row["owner_type"],
            "x": float(row["x"]),
            "y": float(row["y"]),
            "area_ha": float(row["area_ha"]),
            "current_marsh_index": float(row["current_marsh_index"]),
            "upland_migration_index": float(row["upland_migration_index"]),
            "acquisition_cost": float(row["acquisition_cost"]),
            "annual_maintenance_cost": float(row["annual_maintenance_cost"]),
            "staff_days": float(row["staff_days"]),
            "salinity_resilience": float(row["salinity_resilience"]),
            "migration_corridor": int(row["migration_corridor"]),
            "public_access": int(row["public_access"]),
            "protect_allowed": int(row["protect_allowed"]),
            "restore_allowed": int(row["restore_allowed"]),
            "easement_allowed": int(row["easement_allowed"]),
        }

    features = {}
    for row in feature_rows:
        fid = row["feature_id"]
        features[fid] = {
            "taxon_group": row["taxon_group"],
            "home_region": row["home_region"],
            "conservation_value": float(row["conservation_value"]),
            "vulnerability_weight": float(row["vulnerability_weight"]),
            "base_target_units": float(row["base_target_units"]),
            "priority_min_rate": float(row["priority_min_rate"]),
        }

    scenarios = []
    for row in scenario_rows:
        scenarios.append(
            {
                "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
                "probability": float(row["probability"]),
                "sea_level_stress": float(row["sea_level_stress"]),
                "development_pressure": float(row["development_pressure"]),
                "salinity_shift": float(row["salinity_shift"]),
                "restoration_success": float(row["restoration_success"]),
            }
        )

    regions = {}
    for row in region_rows:
        region = row["region"]
        regions[region] = {
            "min_selected": int(row["min_selected"]),
            "max_selected": int(row["max_selected"]),
            "min_public_access": int(row["min_public_access"]),
            "staff_day_cap": float(row["staff_day_cap"]),
        }

    conflicts = [(row["parcel_id_1"], row["parcel_id_2"], row["reason"]) for row in conflict_rows]

    links_by_feature = defaultdict(list)
    link_value = {}
    for row in link_rows:
        pid = row["parcel_id"]
        fid = row["feature_id"]
        base_link = float(row["base_habitat_units"]) * float(row["connectivity_factor"])
        links_by_feature[fid].append((pid, base_link))
        link_value[(pid, fid)] = base_link

    eligible = []
    eligible_by_parcel = defaultdict(list)
    for pid, pdata in parcels.items():
        if pdata["protect_allowed"]:
            eligible.append((pid, "protect"))
            eligible_by_parcel[pid].append("protect")
        if pdata["restore_allowed"]:
            eligible.append((pid, "restore"))
            eligible_by_parcel[pid].append("restore")
        if pdata["easement_allowed"]:
            eligible.append((pid, "migration_easement"))
            eligible_by_parcel[pid].append("migration_easement")

    priority_features = [fid for fid, fdata in features.items() if fdata["priority_min_rate"] > 0.0]

    return {
        "config": config,
        "actions": actions,
        "parcels": parcels,
        "features": features,
        "scenarios": scenarios,
        "regions": regions,
        "conflicts": conflicts,
        "links_by_feature": dict(links_by_feature),
        "link_value": link_value,
        "eligible": eligible,
        "eligible_by_parcel": dict(eligible_by_parcel),
        "priority_features": priority_features,
    }


def action_quality(parcel: Dict, action: Dict, action_name: str, scenario: Dict) -> float:
    marsh_term = (
        parcel["current_marsh_index"] * action["habitat_multiplier"] / scenario["sea_level_stress"]
    )
    upland_term = (
        parcel["upland_migration_index"] * action["upland_multiplier"] / scenario["development_pressure"]
    )
    salinity_term = parcel["salinity_resilience"] / scenario["salinity_shift"]
    restore_term = scenario["restoration_success"] if action_name == "restore" else 1.0
    return (marsh_term + upland_term) * salinity_term * restore_term


def feature_target(feature: Dict, scenario: Dict) -> float:
    avg_stress = (
        scenario["sea_level_stress"]
        + scenario["development_pressure"]
        + scenario["salinity_shift"]
    ) / 3.0
    target = feature["base_target_units"] * (
        1.0 + (feature["vulnerability_weight"] - 1.0) * (avg_stress - 1.0)
    )
    return max(FEAS_TOL, target)


def derive_parameters(instance: Dict) -> Dict:
    parcels = instance["parcels"]
    actions = instance["actions"]
    features = instance["features"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    eligible = instance["eligible"]
    links_by_feature = instance["links_by_feature"]

    costs = {}
    maintenance = {}
    staff = {}
    nursery = {}
    quality = {}
    target = {}
    max_possible_cover = {}

    for pid, action_name in eligible:
        pdata = parcels[pid]
        adata = actions[action_name]

def main() -> int:
    start = time.time()
    env_limit = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    command = "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python /app/submissions/solve.py"
    if env_limit is not None:
        command = f"ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS={env_limit} " + command
    time_limit = get_time_limit()

    instance = load_instance()
    params = derive_parameters(instance)
    phase_results = []

    base_budget = min(30.0, max(10.0, 0.12 * time_limit))
    base_model, base_xvars = build_base_feasibility_model(instance, params, base_budget)
    base_start = time.time()
    base_model.optimize()
    base_runtime = time.time() - base_start
    base_status = str(base_model.getStatus())
    base_nsols = base_model.getNSols()
    base_obj = (
        base_model.getObjVal()
        if base_nsols > 0 and base_model.getBestSol() is not None
        else float("nan")
    )
    phase_results.append(
        {
            "name": "base_feasibility",
            "status": base_status,
            "runtime": base_runtime,
            "nsols": base_nsols,
            "objective": base_obj,
        }
    )
    if base_nsols <= 0 or base_model.getBestSol() is None:
        write_log(
            command=command,
            time_limit=time_limit,
            runtime=time.time() - start,
            status=base_status,
            nsols=base_nsols,
            solver_obj=float("nan"),
            dual_bound=base_model.getDualbound(),
            gap=base_model.getGap() if hasattr(base_model, "getGap") else float("nan"),
            audit={
                "selected_count": 0,
                "capital": 0.0,
                "maintenance": 0.0,
                "staff": 0.0,
                "nursery": 0.0,
                "corridor_count": 0,
                "action_counts": {},
                "expected_score": 0.0,
                "worst_floor": 0.0,
                "objective": 0.0,
                "max_normalized_residual": float("nan"),
                "violated_group_count": 1,
                "violated_constraint_groups": ["no_common_feasible_incumbent"],
                "integrality_residual": 0.0,
                "decision_universe_coverage": 0.0,
                "scenario_scores": {},
            },
            phases=phase_results,
        )
        raise RuntimeError("Base feasibility MIP did not produce an incumbent.")

    base_solution = extract_solution(base_model, base_xvars)
    base_audit = audit_solution(instance, params, base_solution)

    remaining = max(1.0, time_limit - (time.time() - start))
    repair_model, repair_xvars, repair_zvars, repair_slacks, repair_theta, priority_rhs = build_priority_repair_model(
        instance, params, base_solution, remaining
    )
    add_priority_repair_warm_start(
        repair_model,
        repair_xvars,
        repair_zvars,
        repair_slacks,
        repair_theta,
        base_audit,
        base_solution,
        instance["features"],
        instance["priority_features"],
        priority_rhs,
    )

    repair_start = time.time()
    repair_model.optimize()
    repair_runtime = time.time() - repair_start
    runtime = time.time() - start
    status = str(repair_model.getStatus())
    nsols = repair_model.getNSols()
    phase_results.append(
        {
            "name": "priority_repair",
            "status": status,
            "runtime": repair_runtime,
            "nsols": nsols,
            "objective": repair_model.getObjVal() if nsols > 0 and repair_model.getBestSol() is not None else float("nan"),
        }
    )

    if nsols <= 0 or repair_model.getBestSol() is None:
        write_log(
            command=command,
            time_limit=time_limit,
            runtime=runtime,
            status=status,
            nsols=nsols,
            solver_obj=float("nan"),
            dual_bound=repair_model.getDualbound(),
            gap=repair_model.getGap() if hasattr(repair_model, "getGap") else float("nan"),
            audit={
                **base_audit,
                "violated_constraint_groups": sorted(set(base_audit["violated_constraint_groups"]) | {"priority_repair_no_incumbent"}),
                "violated_group_count": len(set(base_audit["violated_constraint_groups"]) | {"priority_repair_no_incumbent"}),
            },
            phases=phase_results,
        )
        raise RuntimeError("Priority repair MIP did not produce an incumbent.")

    solution = extract_solution(repair_model, repair_xvars)
    write_solution(solution)
    audited_solution = read_solution(SOLUTION_PATH)
    audit = audit_solution(instance, params, audited_solution)

    solver_obj = repair_model.getObjVal()
    dual_bound = repair_model.getDualbound()
    gap = repair_model.getGap()

    write_log(
        command=command,
        time_limit=time_limit,
        runtime=runtime,
        status=status,
        nsols=nsols,
        solver_obj=solver_obj,
        dual_bound=dual_bound,
        gap=gap,
        audit=audit,
        phases=phase_results,
    )

    if audit["violated_group_count"] > 0 or audit["max_normalized_residual"] > FEAS_TOL:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Exported solution failed the independent audit; see solve_log.md for details."
        )

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"ERROR: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)
